11.51. MU—Display Central Complex Memory Use
         The MU keyin displays central complex memory use (in decimal words) on a global
         basis, for an individual run, or for all runs in the system.

11.51.1. Formats
         Global Memory Use

           MU

             Displays global memory use.

         Memory Use by a Specific Run

           MU run-id

             Displays memory use by a specific run.

         Memory Use by All Runs

           MU,ALL

             Displays memory use by all runs.

11.51.2. System Response
         The information displayed summarizes the total system memory use. The information
         is displayed by category. Each category corresponds to storage priorities used by the
         Exec for memory management. If a particular category has no memory in use (such as
         no down memory), the category is not displayed.

         On systems that do not support paging, insignificant fields are not displayed.

         MU
         The response to an MU keyin is as follows:
           ACTIVE: 32,972,800
            NAILED 4,435,968
            LOCKED 188,416
           FREE: 100,720,640
           LOGICALLY AVL: 1,992,134
           RESERVED: 586,661
           DOWN: 134,217,728

           MEMORY UTILIZATION BY CLASS
            ABSOLUTE REAL
           EXEC: 28,356,608 9,977,856




11–132                                                                            7831 0281–034
                                                                                Keyin Reference




           USER: 859,070,464 38,326,272
           FILE: 11,952,128 7,589,888
           QBANK:           769,961,984                 14,589,952

         where:

         ACTIVE                        is the number of words currently used by active runs.
         DOWN                          is the number of words in the down state.
         FREE                          is the number of words ready for immediate use.
         LOCKED                        is the number of words locked in memory that can not be
                                       unloaded or moved (for example, they are the source or
                                       destination of an I/O transfer).
         LOGICALLY AVL                 is the number of words that are part of a working set that can
                                       be unloaded to free additional words or be returned to the
                                       active state without use of I/O.
         MEMORY UTILIZATION BY         is the number of words of memory currently in use by the
         CLASS                         Exec, user programs, memory files, and queue banks
                                       (QBANK).
         NAILED                        is the number of words that cannot be unloaded from real
                                       memory, but can be moved around if the physical memory
                                       page is needed for some other function (for example,
                                       partitioning).
         RESERVED                      is the number of words in the reserved state.

         MU run-id
         The information displayed in response to an MU run-id keyin has one of four formats.
         size is the total size of all banks contained in the program and activity level bank
         descriptor tables (BDT).

           run-id size *prog-name/prog-version

            The run is using a program with this name and version (if any). The asterisk (*)
            indicates that the program is loaded from a Transaction Processing (TIP) program
            library file.

           run-id size ** NONE **

            No program is currently in use in this run.

           run-id size NAME$

            The run is using an unnamed program.

           run-id NOT FOUND IN MEMORY

            There is no current run with this run-ID.




7831 0281–034                                                                                  11–133
Keyin Reference




         MU,ALL
         In response to an MU,ALL keyin, the system displays a line of information for each
         run-ID in one of the formats just described.

         If there are insufficient system resources to handle the MU,ALL keyin, the following
         message is displayed:

           MU KEYIN ABORTED - INSUFFICIENT RESOURCES

         If there are no runs in memory at the time of the MU,ALL keyin, the following
         message is displayed:

           NO RUNS IN MEMORY


11.51.3. Examples
         Example 1
         (Systems that support paging)
           MU

            The system responds with
                ACTIVE: 32,972,800
                 NAILED 4,435,968
                 LOCKED 188,416
                FREE: 100,720,640
                DOWN: 134,217,728

                MEMORY UTILIZATION BY CLASS
                 ABSOLUTE REAL
                EXEC: 28,356,608 9,977,856
                USER: 859,070,464 38,326,272
                FILE: 11,952,128 7,589,888
                QBANK: 769,961,984 14,589,952

         (Systems that do not support paging)

                MEMORY UTILIZATION BY CLASS
                 ABSOLUTE
                EXEC: 69,105,860,608
                USER: 859,070,464
                FILE: 11,952,128
                QBANK: 769,961,984

         Example 2
           MU *TIPMO

            The system responds with
                *TIPMO 65536 NAME$

                                                            Keyin Reference

            The system responds with
                BJP 14784 *PMD
